28
In Sept. 2017, an issue was observed in F2 (aRTie) systems: AlRef measured after a baseline on Si is about 0.5% too low.
 This may correspond to changes made in the optics with the introduction of new parabolic mirrors and
 may indicate an error in the formula used to correct the non-linearity and/or in the method of 
 determining the correction coefficients. This amplitude factor determines the amount of correction
 that will result when the baseline linearity correction is calibrated. For systems built in September
 2017 or later this factor should be ~ 0.85. For systems built earlier, calibrations were taken
 using a value of 1. For F10-RT systems, the value of 1.0 seems to work.
In Oct. 2017, an issue was observed in F2 (aRTie) systems: Al2O3 measured after a baseline on Si is about 0.5% too high.
 We found we had not accounted for additional transmitted light reflected off the sample and then off the reference.
 After accounting for this extra light, we found that the correction results in transmittance spectra 
 that is ~ 0.7% low. This amplitude factor allows tuning the amount of correction to apply. For systems built
 in September2017 or later this factor should be ~ 0.368. For systems built earlier, corrections were made
 using a value of 0. For F10-RT systems, the value of 0 seems to work.
